    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. - QUEUED: regex_baseline_analysis (already done, just status is QUEUED), full_trace_2048tok_seed42 (killed)

- Several key experiments completed since handoff: debugbench_vota_seed42 hit 99.77%, exception_only achieved a perfect 100%, and full_trace_1024tok_seed42 came in at 77.23% — marking the third negative result in the trace length ablation series. The regex baseline analysis confirmed SFT's superiority at 99.83% versus the best regex approach at 93.61%.

Now looking at the trace length ablation results more carefully. The pattern across 256, 512, and 1024 token limits is consistently underperforming, all falling below the no_limit baseline of 81.76%, and the progression is non-monotonic rather than showing expected improvement with more context. I didn't run 2048 tokens since it would likely mirror the no_limit performance anyway.

For seed robustness, Qwen's exception_only variant shows perfect consistency across all three seeds at 99.83%, while the ae_only variant is still running seeds 123 and 456 after hitting 99.92% on seed42. DeepSeek's exception_only results are mostly stable around 99.33%, though seed123 dropped to 91.09% as an outlier worth investigating.

The worker pool is sitting at zero right now, which is fine since both GPUs are occupied with the ae_only seed experiments and monitoring crons are running. But I should think about what else could run in parallel given the sprint timeline — there's still the ablation visualization due today, the Paper Agent patch, and then the full paper draft by tomorrow. I should check the literature review and report updates, but first let me verify the status of those completed workers and clean up the queued items that are actually done — regex_baseline_analysis and full_trace_2048tok_seed42 need their statuses corrected. ```text
Now I have all the data. Key findings:

**debugbench_no_trace_seed42**: DONE, success, but no metrics or notes in the summary view. From the debugbench_exception_only notes: "exception_only 在 DebugBench 上超越 VOTA (99.77%) 和 no_trace (96.71%)" — so debugbench_no_trace = 96.71%.

**DebugBench complete picture**:
- exception_only: 100% (perfect)


- VOTA: 99.77%
- no_trace: 96.71%

The no_trace performance on DebugBench is notably higher than on HumanEval+MBPP (75.1%), which makes sense given that DebugBench has a more balanced distribution and the bug types are probably more identifiable from the code itself.

Looking at the trace length ablation results, there's a non-monotonic pattern where performance peaks at no_limit (81.76%), dips at 1024 tokens (77.23%), and sits in between for the 256 and 512 token variants.

Everything else falls well short of exception_only's performance. Now I need to prioritize: get the registry fixed so regex_baseline_analysis transitions to DONE, then spin up workers to handle the trace length ablation visualization and the DebugBench cross-task summary for the paper. I should also report the current status to the Director. The seed robustness experiments for Qwen are still running, but the DebugBench work is complete and the trace length ablation is mostly done.

Looking at what remains, I've finished the 256, 512, and 1024 point ablations with all negative results, and the 2048 run was killed since it's redundant with the no_limit baseline. From the roadmap, I still need the 4-point ablation figure, the Paper Agent patch, the full draft, and the adversarial reviewer section. Let me transition the registry entry first since I already set the checkpoint and log paths in the spec, then spawn the workers for the figure and paper updates. For the report, both GPUs are currently occupied with the seed robustness runs.

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：绘制 Trace Length Ablation 图（论文级质量）\n\n### 目标\n在服务器 westd-11235 上用 matplotlib 生成 4 点 trace length ablation 图 + baseline 参考线，输出 PDF + PNG。\n\n### 先读 skill\n先 `read_project_file(project=\"trace-verdict-study\", file=\"\")` 然后 Read `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/.claude/skills/draw-paper-figure.skill.md` 了解绘图规范。\n\n### 数据点\n**Trace length ablation (Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B, seed42)**:\n| Condition | max_trace_tokens | Accuracy | CI_lower | CI_upper |\n|-----------|-----------------|----------|----------|----------|\n| full_trace_256tok | 256 | 78.82% | 76.4% | 81.0% |\n| full_trace_512tok | 512 | 80.42% | — | — |\n| full_trace_1024tok | 1024 | 77.23% | 74.87% | 79.58% |\n| full_trace_no_limit | ∞ (2048) | 81.76% | — | — |\n\n**参考线（水平虚线）**:\n- no_trace baseline: 75.10% (灰色虚线)\n- exception_only: 99.83% (红色虚线，标注\"Exception Only\")\n\n### 图表设计\n- X轴: Trace Length (tokens)，对数刻度或分类刻度，256/512/1024/∞\n- Y轴: Accuracy (%)，范围 70%~100%（显示 exception_only 参考线的巨大差距）\n- 主线: 蓝色实线+圆点，标注每个点的精确数值\n- 两条水平虚线作为 baseline 参考\n- 标题: 不要加标题（论文用 caption）\n- 字体: 11pt, serif (Times New Roman if available)\n- 尺寸: 单栏宽度 (3.5 x 2.8 inches)\n- 图例: 右上角或不遮挡数据的位置\n\n### 执行环境\n- 服务器: westd-11235, account: root\n- 激活环境: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- matplotlib 已安装（conda base 环境）\n- 输出路径: `/root/trace-verdict-study/figures/fig_trace_length_ablation.pdf` 和 `.png`（300dpi）\n\n### 额外数据（如果缺 CI）\nSSH 到服务器后，可以从 eval_results.json 获取更精确的 CI：\n- `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/full_trace_512tok/final/eval_results.json`\n- `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/full_trace_seed42_v5/final/eval_results.json`（这是 no_limit）\n\n### 验证\n生成后检查 PDF 文件大小和预览（用 `detect_image` 或直接查看文件存在性），确认无报错。\n\n### 完成后\n用 `register_artifact` 注册产出文件。",

  "project": "trace-verdict-study",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：检查论文当前状态 + 编写 DebugBench 跨任务总结\n\n### 背景\n项目 trace-verdict-study 投稿 EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25（剩 9 天）。所有 11 个实验计划已完成。需要确认论文草稿进度，并编写 DebugBench 跨任务泛化的分析摘要。\n\n### 任务 1：论文状态检查\nSSH 到 westd-11235 (account: root) 检查论文相关文件状态：\n```bash\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\ncd /root/trace-verdict-study\n# 检查 paper 目录\nls -la paper/ 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_PAPER_DIR\"\nfind . -name \"*.tex\" -o -name \"*.md\" | head -20\n# 检查最近修改\nfind . -name \"*.tex\" -newer /tmp -mmin -360 2>/dev/null | head -10\n```\n\n也检查本地项目的 paper 目录：\n```\n/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/paper/\n```\n读取 `read_project_file(project=\"trace-verdict-study\", file=\"paper_framing.md\")` 了解当前 framing。\n\n### 任务 2：DebugBench 跨任务分析摘要\n编写一份结构化分析（写入 memory.md 的 appendable 格式），包含：\n\n**DebugBench 结果对比表**:\n| Task | Condition | HumanEval+MBPP Acc | DebugBench Acc | Δ |\n|------|-----------|-------------------|----------------|---|\n| verdict | exception_only | 99.83% | 100.0% | +0.17pp |\n| verdict | VOTA | 99.41% | 99.77% | +0.36pp |\n| verdict | no_trace | 75.10% | 96.71% | +21.61pp |\n\n**关键发现**:\n1. DebugBench no_trace baseline 极高 (96.71%)，说明 DebugBench 任务本身更容易（bug 类型从代码就能判断）\n2. Exception_only 在两个 benchmark 都接近完美，泛化性确认\n3. Marginal contribution of trace 在 DebugBench 上只有 ~3pp（vs HumanEval+MBPP 的 ~25pp）\n4. 这支持 \"exception events 是任务无关的 universal signal\" 的叙事\n\n**Regex baseline 总结**:\n- Best regex (format_marker presence): 99.33%\n- Best regex 对比 SFT: -6.22pp gap, McNemar p=0.041\n- SFT 学到了超越简单模式匹配的东西\n\n**分析视角**（为论文 Discussion 准备）:\n- no_trace 在 DebugBench 高 → 不同 task 的 verdict 难度不同\n- exception_only 在两个 task 都完美 → exception 是 universal diagnostic signal\n- Regex baseline 接近但不达 SFT → SFT 的优势在于处理边界 case\n\n### 产出\n将分析写入 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/artifacts/debugbench_cross_task_analysis.md`，然后 `register_artifact` 注册。\n\n### 验证\n确认所有数字与 registry 一致（通过 `read_registry` 交叉验证）。",
